local Tool = script.Parent;
local Ball = Tool.Handle
local ms = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

enabled = true
function onButton1Down(mouse)
	if not enabled then
		return
	end

	enabled = false
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"

	wait(.2)
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	enabled = true

end

function onEquippedLocal(mouse)


	if mouse == nil then
		print("Mouse not found")
		return 
	end
	Tool.Enabled = true

	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() onButton1Down(mouse) end)
end

Tool.Enabled = true
Tool.Activated:connect(function()
	if ms == nil or not Tool.Enabled then
		return
	end
	local character = Tool.Parent;
	local humanoid = character.Humanoid
	if humanoid == nil then
		print("Humanoid not found")
		return 
	end
	local targetPos = ms.Hit.p
	local lookAt = (targetPos - character.Head.Position).unit
	Tool.ThrowBall:FireServer(lookAt)
	wait(2)
end)


Tool.Equipped:connect(onEquippedLocal)

--Made by Luckymaxer

Mouse_Icon = "rbxasset://textures/GunCursor.png"
Reloading_Icon = "rbxasset://textures/GunWaitCursor.png"

Tool = script.Parent

Mouse = nil

function UpdateIcon()
	if Mouse then
		Mouse.Icon = Tool.Enabled and Mouse_Icon or Reloading_Icon
	end
end

function OnEquipped(ToolMouse)
	Mouse = ToolMouse
	UpdateIcon()
end

function OnChanged(Property)
	if Property == "Enabled" then
		UpdateIcon()
	end
end

Tool.Equipped:connect(OnEquipped)
Tool.Changed:connect(OnChanged)f

spraysize = 5
cooldown = 1.9
baseid = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendSprayPaint.OnClientEvent:connect(function(plr,sprayid, mousepos, surface, target)
	if target == nil then return end
	local canspray=false
	if (surface==Enum.NormalId.Front or surface==Enum.NormalId.Back) and target.Size.x>=spraysize and target.Size.y>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	elseif (surface==Enum.NormalId.Left or surface==Enum.NormalId.Right) and target.Size.y>=spraysize and target.Size.z>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	elseif (surface==Enum.NormalId.Top or surface==Enum.NormalId.Bottom) and target.Size.x>=spraysize and target.Size.z>=spraysize then
		canspray=true
	end
	if canspray then
		local p=Instance.new("Part")
		local d=Instance.new("Decal")
		d.Texture=baseid..tostring(sprayid-1)
		d.Face=surface
		d.Parent=p
		p.Name="!SPRAY_"..plr.Name
		p.formFactor="Custom"
		p.Anchored=false
		p.CanCollide=false
		p.Transparency=1
		p.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01,1,1)
		if surface==Enum.NormalId.Front or surface==Enum.NormalId.Back then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(spraysize,spraysize,.2)
		elseif surface==Enum.NormalId.Left or surface==Enum.NormalId.Right then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(.2,spraysize,spraysize)
		elseif surface==Enum.NormalId.Top or surface==Enum.NormalId.Bottom then
			p.Size=Vector3.new(spraysize,.2,spraysize)
		end
		local w=Instance.new("Weld")
		w.Part0=target
		w.Part1=p
		local cf=CFrame.new(target.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(mousepos))
		w.C0=cf
		w.C1=CFrame.new(0,0,0)
		w.Parent=target
		p.CFrame=target.CFrame:toWorldSpace(cf)		--this is here to position the spray other wise it will be at 0,0,0 if the target is anchored
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p,60)
		p.Parent=game.Workspace
	end
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local gui = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local container = gui:WaitForChild("ContextActionsGui")

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local playerstatus = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("SpecialStatus")

local CurrentObject = nil
local actions = {}

local hover_icon = "rbxassetid://231195429"
local normal_icon = "rbxasset://textures/ArrowFarCursor.png"
local select_sound = "rbxassetid://231188062"

--[[

on RenderStepped: 

IF NO TOOL EQUIPPED:


IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- get mouse Hit. if the object's ContextActionsModelName is the same as the CurrentObjectHoveringOver, change nothing.
- if ContextActionsModelName is different (or there is currently a CurrentObjectHoveringOver, and a new ContextActionsModelName is not found), then remove the "Interact with" overlay if there is one and set mouse icon to White. if there is a ContextActionsModelName in the new object, make that the overlay and set mouse icon to Green.

- every frame of this, update any "Interact with" overlay's position


IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is outside of context menu's range, remove context menu




otherwise, IF TOOL EQUIPPED: 
- remove any overlays, do not change mouse icon







on MouseButton1Down:
IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is over object, generate the object's action menu. set mouse icon to White

IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is not over context menu, remove context menu

each action button has its own Clicked connection which will run the action's module and then remove the context menu

]]

function isOffScreen(point,bx,by) --bx and by are part of AbsoluteSize
	if point.x < 0 or point.x > bx or point.y < 0 or point.y > by then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function checkOffScreenCorners(element) --corners are like quadrants
	local pos = element.Position
	local size = element.Size
	local c1 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c2 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c3 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local c4 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local bx = container.AbsoluteSize.x
	local by = container.AbsoluteSize.y
	return isOffScreen(c1,bx,by),isOffScreen(c2,bx,by), isOffScreen(c3,bx,by),isOffScreen(c4,bx,by)
end

function handsAreFree()
	if plr.Character then
		for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("HopperBin") then
				return false
			end
		end
		for i,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

function getAvailableActions(obj)
	local getactions = {}
	local objname = obj:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
	if objname then
		objname = objname.Value
		local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if objdata then
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("StringValue") then
					table.insert(getactions,v)
				end
			end
			
			local owner = obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if owner then
				if owner.Value == plr.Name then
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("StringValue") then
							table.insert(getactions,v)
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
						if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then --if this status name is inside the player's special statuses
							for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do --add any actions that are under that category
								table.insert(getactions,x)
							end
						end
					end
				else
					if owner.Value == "" then
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.NoOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					else
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.OtherOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for i,v in pairs(objdata.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata.NotSpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if not playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
	actions = getactions
end

gui:WaitForChild("ActionMenuSelection").Event:connect(function(num)
	if CurrentObject ~= nil then
		local objname = CurrentObject:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
		if objname then
			objname = objname.Value
			local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
			if objdata then
				local action = objdata:FindFirstChild(num,true)
				if action then
					local module = require(action.ModuleScript)
					module.RunAction(plr,CurrentObject) --run the action now!
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local RMBdown = false

function WithinValidDistance(hit,target)
	local model = target.Parent
	local maxdistance = 32
	if model:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsDistance") then
		maxdistance = model.ContextActionsDistance.Value
	end
	if char and hrp and (hrp.Position - hit.p).magnitude < maxdistance then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function MouseOverActiveFrame(x,y)
	local isover = false
	local function recurse(instance)
		for _,v in pairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
			if (v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and v.Visible == true then
				if (x > v.AbsolutePosition.x and x < v.AbsolutePosition.x + v.AbsoluteSize.x and y > v.AbsolutePosition.y and y < v.AbsolutePosition.y + v.AbsoluteSize.y) then
					isover = true
					break
				else
					recurse(v)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if gui:FindFirstChild("MainMenu") then
		recurse(gui.MainMenu)
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("PhoneGUI") and gui.PhoneGUI.Frame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.PhoneGUI)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("HouseEditMode") and gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
	local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
	if handsAreFree() then
		if not actionmenu then --manage interact overlay if there's no action menu
			local targetmodel
			if mouse.Target then
				targetmodel = (mouse.Target.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") or mouse.Target.Parent:IsA("Accoutrement") and mouse.Target.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName"))
			end
			if targetmodel then
				targetmodel = targetmodel.Parent
			end
			if mouse.Target and targetmodel and not RMBdown and WithinValidDistance(mouse.Hit,mouse.Target) and not MouseOverActiveFrame(mouse.X,mouse.Y) then
				if targetmodel ~= CurrentObject then --if object is valid and it is not the same one being hovered over already, get actions
					getAvailableActions(targetmodel)
					if #actions > 0 then
						CurrentObject = targetmodel
						if overlay then
							overlay:Destroy()
						end
						mouse.Icon = hover_icon
						overlay = container.BaseInteractOverlay:clone()
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Name = "InteractOverlay"
						if #actions == 1 then
							overlay.Text = actions[1].Value
						else
							local modelname = targetmodel.ContextActionsModelName.Value
							if modelname == "Player" then
								modelname = targetmodel.Name
							end
							overlay.Text = "Interact with " ..modelname
						end
						overlay.Parent = container
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Size = UDim2.new(0,overlay.TextBounds.X + 15,0,16)
					end
				end
			else --remove overlay if not hovering over valid object - either no CAMN, too far away, hovering over active GUI frame, or RMB is down
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				CurrentObject = nil
				if overlay then
					overlay:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if overlay then
				overlay.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y - overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(overlay)
				if c4 == true and c1 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position - UDim2.new(0,overlay.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
				end
				if c1 == true and c2 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				end
			end
		else
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset - 40 or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset + 40 or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset - 40 or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset + 40 then
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	else
		if actionmenu then
			actionmenu:Destroy()
		end
		if overlay then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
		CurrentObject = nil
	end
end)

mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if not actionmenu then 
			
			if CurrentObject ~= nil and #actions > 0 then --create an action menu for the object, then set mouse icon
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",gui)
				sound.SoundId = select_sound
				sound:Play()
				sound.TimePosition = .05
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.5)
				if #actions >= 2 then
					mouse.Icon = normal_icon
					local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
					if overlay then
						overlay:Destroy()
					end
					actionmenu = container.BaseActionMenu:clone()
					actionmenu.Name = "ActionMenu"
					actionmenu.Parent = container
					actionmenu.Visible = true
					local maxwidth = 0
					for i,v in pairs(actions) do
						local button = actionmenu.BaseSelector:clone()
						button.Name = v.Name
						button.Text = " "..v.Value
						button.Parent = actionmenu
						button.Visible = true
						button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0, (#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 2) * 20)
						if button.TextBounds.X > maxwidth then
							maxwidth = button.TextBounds.X
						end
						
						if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") and i == 1 then
							game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
						end
					end
					actionmenu.Size = UDim2.new(0,maxwidth + 3,0,(#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 1) * 20)
					actionmenu.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y)
					local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(actionmenu)
					if c1 == true and c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,0,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c1 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
					end
				else
					gui.ActionMenuSelection:Fire(actions[1].Name) --only one action
				end
			end
			
		else --action menu already open - is mouse in range of it?
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Down:connect(function() --remove on right click outside of menu
	RMBdown = true
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if actionmenu then 
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Up:connect(function()
	RMBdown = false
end)

if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	
	UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then

				end
			end
		end
	end)

script.Parent.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "InstructionsGui" then
		if child:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
			child.Frame.Visible = false
		end
		wait(1/60)
		if child and child:FindFirstChild("Frame") and child:FindFirstChild("MobileControl") then
			child.MobileControl:Destroy()
			child.Frame:Destroy()
			
			local platform = "PC"
			if uis.TouchEnabled then
				platform = "Mobile"
			elseif plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
				platform = "Xbox"
			end
			
			local newframe = script.InstructionsGui[platform.."Frame"]:clone()
			newframe.Name = "Frame"
			newframe.Visible = true
			newframe.Parent = child
			local newscript = script.InstructionsGui.MobileControl:clone()
			newscript.Parent = child
			newscript.Disabled = false
		end
	end
end)

=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=504207 runSha256=e97f192bb86838f789561443c748bc23ec86fb222fa73f835a43b3504db04a9d ===
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local enabled = true
local playermouse

local range = script.Parent:WaitForChild("TeleportRange").Value

local cd = 2

local localeffect = plr.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TeleportEffect")

function standUp(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		char.Humanoid.Jump = true
	end
end

function checkIfStanding(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		if char.Humanoid.SeatPart == nil then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end

function enable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = true
end

function disable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = false
end

function activate(targetpos,mag)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and enabled then
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if torso and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			disable()
			
			local origin = torso.Position
			local ray1 = Ray.new(origin, (targetpos - origin).unit * mag)
			
			local ignorelist = {}
	
			for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Character then
					table.insert(ignorelist,v.Character)
				end
			end
			
			local hit,destination
			local collision = false
			
			hit,destination = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray1,ignorelist)
			if hit then
				destination = destination - ray1.Unit.Direction * 1.5
				collision = true
			end
			
			if collision then
				local neworigin = targetpos + Vector3.new(0,17,0)
				local ray2 = Ray.new(neworigin, ((neworigin - Vector3.new(0,17,0)) - neworigin).unit * 16)
				local hit2,destination2
				hit2,destination2 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray2, ignorelist)
				if not hit2 then
					destination = targetpos
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayerHasTeleported:FireServer(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			
			localeffect:Fire(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			if checkIfStanding(char) == false then
				standUp(char)
			end
			wait(.15)
			if torso and checkIfStanding(char) == true then
				torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination)
			end
			
			wait(cd)
			
			enable()
			
		end
	end
end


script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	playermouse = mouse
	equipped = true
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if enabled and equipped then
			local char = plr.Character
			if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				
				local mag = (char.HumanoidRootPart.Position - (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0))).magnitude
				if mag > range then
					mag = range
				end
				local ray = Ray.new(char.HumanoidRootPart.Position, (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag)
				local target = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position + (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag

				activate(target,mag)
			end
			
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()

		lastposition = part.Position
	end

local selectionBox
local selectionLasso

local selectedButtonTable = {}
local oldButton = nil
function onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	if oldButton ~= nil then
		local notSelected = true
		local selectionText = ""
		for key, value in pairs(selectedButtonTable) do
			if oldButton == value then
				notSelected = false
			else
				selectionText = value.BackgroundColor.Name
			end
		end
		if notSelected then
			hoverSelection.Text = selectionText
			oldButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
		end
	end
	oldButton = nil
end

function onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton)
	onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	hoverSelection.Text = guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name
	if guiButton ~= selectedButton then
		guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
		oldButton = guiButton
	end
end

function onMouseUp(colorHolder, paletteFrame, guiButton)
	if selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] ~= nil then
		selectedButtonTable[colorHolder].Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	end
	guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Yellow()
	colorHolder.BackgroundColor = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionBox.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionLasso.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	Tool.Value.Value = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] = guiButton
	
	onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame)
	
	print("Selected Color: " .. guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name)
end

function onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame)
	paletteFrame.Visible = true
end

function setSelectionBox(part) 
	unsetSelectionBox()
	selectionBox.Adornee = part.Parent
	selectionLasso.Part = part.Parent.PrimaryPart
end
function unsetSelectionBox() 
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
end

function onMouseEnterGui(mouse)
	mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\ArrowCursor.png"
	unsetSelectionBox()
	inGui = true
end
function onMouseLeaveGui(mouse)
	inGui = false
end

function onMouseEnterPalette(mouse)
	mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\ArrowCursor.png"
	unsetSelectionBox()
	inPalette = true
end
function onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame, mouse)
	paletteFrame.Visible = false
	inPalette = false
end
	

local primaryColor = nil
function buildGui(root, mouse)

	local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1.0, -200, 1.0, -250)
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.0, 200, 0.0, 250)
	mainFrame.Transparency = 1.0
	mainFrame.Parent = root
	

	local paletteFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	paletteFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, -0.8, 0)
	paletteFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, 0, 8.0/5, 0)
	paletteFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	paletteFrame.Visible = false
	paletteFrame.Parent = mainFrame
	paletteFrame.MouseEnter:connect(function() print("EnterPalette") onMouseEnterPalette(mouse) end)
	paletteFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() print("LeavePalette")  onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame, mouse) end)

	local sideBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	sideBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, 4.0/5, 0)
	sideBar.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, 0, 1.0/5, 0)
	sideBar.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	sideBar.Parent = mainFrame
	sideBar.MouseEnter:connect(function() onMouseEnterGui(mouse) end)
	sideBar.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeaveGui(mouse) end)

	primaryColor = Instance.new("TextButton")
	primaryColor.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 1, 0.0, 1)
	primaryColor.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, -2, 1.0, -2)
	primaryColor.Text  = ""	
	primaryColor.BackgroundColor = Tool.Value.Value
	primaryColor.BorderColor = BrickColor.Black()
	primaryColor.BorderSizePixel = 1
	primaryColor.Parent = sideBar
	primaryColor.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() print("Showdialog") onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame) end)


	local hoverSelection = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	hoverSelection.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, 0.0, 0)
	hoverSelection.Size = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 1.0, 0)
	hoverSelection.Text = ""
	hoverSelection.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	hoverSelection.TextColor = BrickColor.White()
	hoverSelection.Text = primaryColor.BackgroundColor.Name
	hoverSelection.Parent = sideBar

	--local secondaryColor = Instance.new("TextButton")
	--secondaryColor.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0.0, 0)
	--secondaryColor.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 1.0, 0)
	--secondaryColor.Text = ""
	--secondaryColor.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	--secondaryColor.Parent = sideBar

	for xOffset = 0, 7 do
		for yOffset = 0,15 do
			local guiFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
			guiFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1.0/8 * xOffset, 0, 1.0/16*yOffset, 0)
			guiFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1.0/8, 0, 1.0/16, 0)
			guiFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
			guiFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			guiFrame.Parent = paletteFrame
		
			local guiButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
			guiButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 2, 0.0, 2)
			guiButton.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, -4, 1.0, -4)
			guiButton.Text = ""
			guiButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
			guiButton.AutoButtonColor = false
			guiButton.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.palette(xOffset + yOffset*8)
			guiButton.MouseEnter:connect(function() onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton) end)
			guiButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() onMouseUp(primaryColor, paletteFrame, guiButton) end)
			--guiButton.MouseButton2Down:connect(function() onMouseClick(secondaryColor, guiButton) end)
			guiButton.Parent = guiFrame

			if guiButton.BackgroundColor == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
				guiFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
				selectedButtonTable[primaryColor] = guiButton
			end
		end
	end
	
	local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
		local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
		local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
		
		UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)
			if sideBar and primaryColor and sideBar.Parent ~= nil and paletteFrame and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and not gameprocessed then
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					else
						paletteFrame.Visible = true
						local toselect = primaryColor
						for _,v in pairs(paletteFrame:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Frame") and v:FindFirstChild("TextButton") then
								if v.TextButton.BackgroundColor3 == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
									toselect = v
								end
							end
						end
						GuiService.SelectedObject = toselect
					end
				elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	
	mainFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeave(hoverSelection) end)

end

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.ArtPainting)

function canSelectCanvas(part)
	if part ~= nil then
		return (part.Position - Tool.Parent.Head.Position).Magnitude < 16
	end
end

function Paint(canvas,position)
	
	if canSelectCanvas(canvas) then
		--primaryColor.BackgroundColor
		module.PlacePaint(game.Players.LocalPlayer, canvas, position, mode.Value, script.Parent.Value.Value)
		
	end
end

local mouseDown = false
function on3dButton1Down(mouse) 
	mouseDown = true
	while mouseDown do
		local canvas = mouse.Target
		local pos = mouse.Hit.p
		if canvas and canvas.Name == "!Canvas" then
			if lastposition == nil or (lastposition - pos).magnitude > .033 then
				Paint(canvas,pos)
			end
		else
			mouseDown = false
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
	
end

function on3dButton1Up(mouse) 
	mouseDown = false
	lastposition = nil
end



function on3dMouseMove(mouse) 
	if not(inGui) and not(inPalette) then
		--[[mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\FillCursor.png"
		local part = mouse.Target

		if mouseDown then
			setColor(part)
		end]]
	end
end

local guiMain
function onEquippedLocal(mouse)
	
	local character = script.Parent.Parent
	local player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	
	PBGui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("PaintbrushGui"):clone()
	PBGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui
	PBGui.AttachedTool.Value = script.Parent

	guiMain = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	guiMain.Name = "PaintBucketGui"
	guiMain.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	inGui = false
	inPalette = false
	mouseDown = false
	
	local uis = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	if (not uis.TouchEnabled) and (uis.KeyboardEnabled or uis.GamepadEnabled) then
		mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() on3dButton1Down(mouse) end)
		mouse.Button1Up:connect(function() on3dButton1Up(mouse) end)
	end
	
	mouse.Move:connect(function() on3dMouseMove(mouse) end)
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	mouse.TargetFilter = workspace.ArtCanvases

	selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
	selectionBox.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionBox.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	selectionLasso = Instance.new("SelectionPartLasso")
	selectionLasso.Name = "Model Delete Lasso"
	selectionLasso.Humanoid = character.Humanoid
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
	selectionLasso.Visible = true
	selectionLasso.archivable = false
	selectionLasso.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionLasso.Parent = game.workspace
	
	buildGui(guiMain, mouse)
end

function onUnequippedLocal()
	guiMain:Remove()
	selectionBox:Remove()
	selectionLasso:Remove()
	if PBGui then
		PBGui:Destroy()
	end
end

		local mouseisdown = emulation:WaitForChild("MouseIsDown")
		local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
		while true do
			wait(1/31)

				local canvas = mouse.Target
				local pos = mouse.Hit.p

						cameralock.Name = "CameraLock"
						game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(cameralock,1)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

--imports
local RbxUtility = LoadLibrary('RbxUtility')
local Create = RbxUtility.Create

--main vars
local Tool = script.Parent
local Equipped = false
local Flying = false

--character stuff
local Character, Player, Humanoid, Torso;

--anim tracks
local GliderHold, GliderFly, GliderBomb;


---========================== main code ==========================--

local FlyingGyro;
local FlyingForce;

local DesiredFlightCFrame;

local Gui;

local MobileCooldown = false --can't start again immediately after jumping
local UnequipCooldown = false
local AboutToFly = false
local IsMobile = false

local function BeginFlight()
	if Flying or MobileCooldown or not Equipped or AboutToFly then return end
	Humanoid.Jump = true
	AboutToFly = true
	wait(0.2)
	AboutToFly = false
	if Flying or not Equipped then return end
	Flying = true
	UnequipCooldown = true
	if not IsMobile then
		game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Tool.Handle
		game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = 'Track'
	end
	Humanoid.Sit = true
	FlyingGyro = Create'BodyGyro'{
		Parent = Torso;
		maxTorque = Vector3.new(0,0,0);
		cframe = Torso.CFrame;
	}
	FlyingForce = Create'BodyForce'{
		Parent = Torso;
		force = Vector3.new(0,0,0);
	}
	Spawn(function()
		for i = 0, 1, 0.1 do
			FlyingGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(i*10000,i*10000,i*10000)
		end
	end)
	GliderFly:Play(0.3)
	DesiredFlightCFrame = (Torso.CFrame - Torso.CFrame.p)*CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0.01)
	Gui.Frame.Message.Text = --Fix implemented by Luckymaxer
		"Click and Hold to direct yourself.\n"..
		"Jump to stop gliding."
	wait(1)
	UnequipCooldown = false
end

local function EndFlight(forceremove)
	if UnequipCooldown == false or forceremove then
		Flying = false
		MobileCooldown = true
		if not IsMobile then
			game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Character
			game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = 'Custom'
		end
		GliderFly:Stop(0.3)
		Humanoid.Sit = false
		FlyingGyro:Destroy()
		FlyingForce:Destroy()
		--Torso.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		Torso.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(Torso.Position)
		Gui.Frame.Message.Text = "Click to Glide."
		wait(1)
		MobileCooldown = false
	end
end


local function GetMassOf(model)
	if model:IsA('BasePart') then
		return model:GetMass()
	else
		local mass = 0
		for _, ch in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
			mass = mass + GetMassOf(ch)
		end
		return mass
	end
end


local LastBombAt = tick()
local RELOAD_TIME = 0.8
local function DropBomb()
	if not Flying then return end
	if tick() - LastBombAt > RELOAD_TIME then
		LastBombAt = tick()
		--
		GliderBomb:Play()
		--
		local bomb = Create'Part'{
			Size = Vector3.new(1,2,1);
			TopSurface = 'Smooth';
			BottomSurface = 'Smooth';
			Archivable = false;
			Position = (Character['Left Arm'].CFrame*CFrame.new(0,-1,0)).p - Vector3.new(0,2,0);
			--
			Create'SpecialMesh'{
				MeshId = 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=90793198';
				TextureId = 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=90793278';
			};
			--
			Create'Sound'{
				Name = 'ExplodeSound';
				SoundId = 'rbxasset://sounds//Rocket shot.wav';
			};
		}
		--
		local sc = Tool.MineScript:Clone()
		sc.Disabled = false
		sc.creator.Value = Player
		sc.Parent = bomb
		--
		bomb.Parent = game.Workspace
	end
end

local function Slerp(t, a, b)
	local om = math.acos(math.min(1, a:Dot(b)))
	if om < 0.01 then
		return (1-t)*a +
		          t *b
	else
		return (math.sin((1-t)*om)/math.sin(om))*a +
		       (math.sin(   t *om)/math.sin(om))*b
	end
end


while not Tool:FindFirstChild('Handle') do wait() end
Tool.Handle.Touched:connect(function(part)
	if Equipped and not part:IsDescendantOf(Character) and Flying then
		EndFlight()
	end
end)

local oldmouse

Tool.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character.Humanoid
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	--
	Equipped = true
	--
	GliderHold = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_Hold)
	if Humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
		GliderFly = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_FlyR15)
	else
		GliderFly =  Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_Fly )
	end
	GliderBomb = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_Bomb)
	--
	GliderHold:Play()
	--
	local MouseDown = false
	local KeyState
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		MouseDown = true
		if not Flying then
			BeginFlight()
		end
	end)
	mouse.Button1Up:connect(function()
		MouseDown = false
	end)
	mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key)
		if key == ' ' then
			if Flying then
				EndFlight()
			end
		elseif key:byte() == 48 or key:byte() == 45 or key:byte() == 50 or 
		       key:byte() == 52 or key:byte() == 13 or key:lower() == 'q' or key:lower() == 'e' 
		then
			--DropBomb()
		end
	end)
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		IsMobile = true
		oldmouse = mouse
		for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
			v.Changed:connect(function(val)
				if Equipped and oldmouse == mouse then
					if v.Name == " " and val == true then
						if Flying then
							EndFlight()
						end
					elseif v.Name == "MouseIsDown" then
						if val == true then
							MouseDown = true
							if not Flying then
								BeginFlight()
							end
						else
							MouseDown = false
						end
					end
				end
				--InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
			end)
		end
	end
	--
	Gui = Create'ScreenGui'{
		Parent = Player.PlayerGui;
		Create'Frame'{
			Name = 'Frame';
			Style = 'RobloxRound';
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -125, 1, -170);
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 80);
			Create'TextLabel'{
				Name = 'Message';
				TextWrapped = true;
				BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				Font = 'Arial';
				FontSize = 'Size14';
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(0.9, 0.9, 0.9);
				Text = "Click to Glide";
				Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0);
			};
		};
	}
	--
	local LastTime = tick()
	while true do
		local Now = tick()
		local dt = Now - LastTime
		LastTime = Now
		--
		if Flying then
			--make me fly
			local moveDir = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame*CFrame.Angles(0.6, 0, 0)
			if MouseDown then
				local theta = math.acos(DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector:Dot(moveDir.lookVector))
				local frac = math.min(1, 1.5*dt/theta)
				local unit = Slerp(frac, DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector, moveDir.lookVector)
				print(unit)
				DesiredFlightCFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0,0,0), unit)
			end
			--
			local velo = Torso.Velocity
			local veloXZ = (velo - Vector3.new(0, velo.y, 0))
			--
			local dir = DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector
			local dirXZ = (dir - Vector3.new(0, dir.y, 0)).unit
			--
			FlyingGyro.cframe = DesiredFlightCFrame*
			                    CFrame.Angles(0, 0, Torso.RotVelocity.y/3 * (1-math.abs(dir.y)^2))*
			                    CFrame.Angles(-math.pi/2, 0, 0)
			--
			local headingForceFactor = -dir.y -- heading up => negative force
			local liftForceFactor = dir.y --hading up => positive lift
			--
			local forwardsSpeed = math.max(0, velo.magnitude)
			--
			local weight = GetMassOf(Character)*20*9.81
			--
			local dragForce = (velo.magnitude/10)^2 * weight
			local dragForceY = (velo.y/15)^2 * weight
			local dragForceXZ = (veloXZ.magnitude/35)^2 * weight
			--
			local suspendFactor = 0
			if dir.y < 0 then
				suspendFactor = (-dir.y)^2
			end
			--
			FlyingForce.force = Vector3.new(0, weight*(1.0 - suspendFactor + 0.3*liftForceFactor), 0)
			                    + (dirXZ * (weight*1.0 * math.max(0, (0.5*headingForceFactor+0.6))))
			                    - (veloXZ.unit * dragForceXZ)
			                    - (Vector3.new(0, velo.y, 0).unit * dragForceY)
			--
			--print(string.format("RotVelocity: %.1f, Velo: %.2f",
			--                    Torso.RotVelocity.y, velo.magnitude))
		end
		wait()
	end
end)

	Equipped = false
	--
	if Gui then
		Gui:Destroy()
	end

	GliderHold:Stop()
	GliderFly:Stop()
	GliderBomb:Stop()

local Tool = script.Parent;
local torso = nil
local human = nil

function SetMouseIcon(mouse,bool)
	if mouse == nil then return end
	mouse.Icon = bool == true and "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png" or "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"
end


function onEquippedLocal(mouse)
	if mouse == nil then
		print("Mouse not found")
		return 
	end

	torso = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	human = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")--getHumanoid(Tool.Parent)
	if torso == nil or human == nil then return end
	
	SetMouseIcon(mouse,Tool.IsEnabled.Value)
	Tool.IsEnabled.Changed:connect(function(bool)
		SetMouseIcon(mouse,bool)
	end)

	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() 
		script.Parent.MouseClick:FireServer(mouse.Hit.p)
	end)
	mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key)
		script.Parent.KeyPress:FireServer(key)
	end)
end

=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=682618 runSha256=5bfef9e841dbebef23ec170db2195f9b6964d83ddd376aa51e037777b859d008 ===
--Made by Stickmasterluke
--[[
	Fixed by ArceusInator.  Now works with FE, fixed an issue with first person allowing the player to phase through walls.  Now runs at 60 FPS.
--]]


sp=script.Parent


speed=2
topspeed=50




function waitfor(parent,name)
	while parent:FindFirstChild(name)==nil do
		wait()
	end
	return parent:FindFirstChild(name)
end

local canim=waitfor(sp,"coastingpose")
local lanim=waitfor(sp,"leftturn")
local ranim=waitfor(sp,"rightturn")
local handle=waitfor(sp,"Handle")
local wind=waitfor(handle,"Wind")
local mesh=waitfor(handle,"Mesh")

inertia=1-(speed/topspeed)
debris=game:GetService("Debris")
equipped=false
flying=false
controls={forward=0,backward=0,left=0,right=0}
momentum=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
lastmomentum=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
totalmomentum=0
tilt=0
lasttilt=0
currentanim="coast"
lastanim=wind
mesh.Scale=Vector3.new(3,3,3)
handle.Size=Vector3.new(2,1.5,3)
sp.Grip=CFrame.new(0,0,0)


function RemoveFlyStuff()
	wind:Stop()
	local plr=game.Players.LocalPlayer
	if plr then
		local chr=plr.Character
		if chr then
			for i,v in ipairs(chr:getChildren()) do
				if v.Name=="Humanoid" then
					v.PlatformStand=false
				end
				if v and v.Name=="EffectCloud" then
					sp.Call:FireServer(v, 'Destroy')
				end
			end
			local torso=chr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if torso~=nil then
				for i,v in ipairs(torso:GetChildren()) do
					if v and (v.Name=="FlightGyro" or v.Name=="FlightVelocity") then
						v:remove()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	handle.Transparency=0
	sp.Set:FireServer(handle.Smoke, 'Enabled', true)
	if lastanim~=nil then
		lastanim:Stop()
	end
end

function fly()		--(de)activate fly mode
	if not equipped then
		flying=false
	else
		flying=not flying
	end
	local plr=game.Players.LocalPlayer
	RemoveFlyStuff()
	if flying then
		local torso=sp.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local humanoid=sp.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if torso and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0 then
			sp.Set:FireServer(handle, 'Transparency', 1)
			sp.Set:FireServer(handle.Smoke, 'Enabled', false)
			humanoid.PlatformStand=true
			humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			momentum=torso.Velocity+(torso.CFrame.lookVector*10)+Vector3.new(0,3,0)

			local gyro=Instance.new("BodyGyro")
			gyro.Name="FlightGyro"
			gyro.P=10^6
			gyro.maxTorque=Vector3.new(gyro.P,gyro.P,gyro.P)
			gyro.cframe=torso.CFrame
			gyro.Parent=torso

			velocity=Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			velocity.Name="FlightVelocity"
			velocity.velocity=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
			velocity.P=10^4
			velocity.maxForce=Vector3.new(1,1,1)*(10^6)
			velocity.Parent=torso
			
			local cloud=sp.Make:InvokeServer('Cloud')

			if lastanim~=nil then
				lastanim:Stop()
			end
			lastanim=humanoid:LoadAnimation(canim)
			lastanimused=0
			if lastanim then
				lastanim:Play(.5)
			end
			sp.Call:FireServer(wind, 'Play')
			while flying and torso and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0 and equipped do
				local cvector = Vector3.new(controls.left+controls.right,math.abs(controls.forward)*.2,controls.forward+controls.backward)
				local movement = Vector3.new()
				if cvector.magnitude > 0 then
					movement=game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame:vectorToWorldSpace(cvector.unit*speed)
				end

				momentum=(momentum*inertia)+movement
				totalmomentum=math.min(momentum.magnitude,topspeed)
				local momentumpercent=(totalmomentum/topspeed)
				if cloud~=nil then
					local m=cloud:FindFirstChild("Mesh")
					sp.Set:FireServer(m, 'Scale', Vector3.new(4,4,4+(momentumpercent*4)))
					m.Scale = Vector3.new(4,4,4+(momentumpercent*4))
				end
				sp.Set:FireServer(wind, 'Pitch', (momentumpercent*2)+1)
				sp.Set:FireServer(wind, 'Volume', momentumpercent+.1)

				local tilt=((momentum*Vector3.new(1,0,1)).unit:Cross(((lastmomentum*Vector3.new(1,0,1)).unit))).y
				if tostring(tilt)=="-1.#IND" or tostring(tilt)=="1.#IND" or tilt==math.huge or tilt==-math.huge or tostring(0/0) == tostring(tilt) then
					tilt=0
				end
				local abstilt=math.abs(tilt)
				if abstilt>.06 or abstilt<.0001 then
					if math.abs(lasttilt)>.0001 then
						tilt=lasttilt*.96
					else
						tilt=0
					end
				else
					tilt=(lasttilt*.9)+(tilt*.1)			--weighted average
				end
				lasttilt=tilt
				if tilt>.01 then
					if lastanimused~=1 then
						lastanim:Stop(.5)
						lastanim=humanoid:LoadAnimation(ranim)
						lastanim:Play(.5)
						lastanimused=1
					end
				elseif tilt<(-.01) then
					if lastanimused~=-1 then
						lastanim:Stop(.5)
						lastanim=humanoid:LoadAnimation(lanim)
						lastanim:Play(.5)
						lastanimused=-1
					end
				elseif lastanimused~=0 then
					lastanim:Stop(.5)
					lastanim=humanoid:LoadAnimation(canim)
					lastanim:Play(.5)
					lastanimused=0
				end
				if totalmomentum<.5 then
					momentum=Vector3.new(0,0,0)
					totalmomentum=0
					gyro.cframe=CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0,0,0),game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector*Vector3.new(1,0,1))*CFrame.Angles(0,-math.pi/2,0)
				else
					gyro.cframe=CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0,0,0),momentum)*CFrame.Angles(0,-math.pi/2,0)*CFrame.Angles(tilt*(-20),0,0)
				end
				velocity.velocity=momentum
				lastmomentum=momentum
				game:GetService'RunService'.RenderStepped:wait()
			end
		end
		humanoid.AutoRotate = true
		RemoveFlyStuff()
		flying=false
	end
end

function onEquipped(mouse)
	equipped=true
	if mouse~=nil then
		mouse.Icon="rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
		local function StartFlying()
			fly()
			local torso=sp.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if torso~=nil then
				torso.Velocity=momentum
			end
		end
		
		local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
		local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
		
		if not Emulation then
			mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
				StartFlying()
			end)
		else
			spawn(StartFlying)
		end
		mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key2)
			local key=string.byte(key2)
			if key==32 then					--Space bar
				--[[fly()
				local torso=sp.Parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if torso~=nil then
					torso.Velocity=momentum
				end]]
			elseif key==string.byte("w") or key==17 then
				controls.forward=-1
			elseif key==string.byte("a") or key==20 then
				controls.left=-1
			elseif key==string.byte("s") or key==18 then
				controls.backward=1
			elseif key==string.byte("d") or key==19 then
				controls.right=1
			end
		end)
		mouse.KeyUp:connect(function(key2)
			local key=string.byte(key2)
			if key==string.byte("w") or key==17 then
				controls.forward=0
			elseif key==string.byte("a") or key==20 then
				controls.left=0
			elseif key==string.byte("s") or key==18 then
				controls.backward=0
			elseif key==string.byte("d") or key==19 then
				controls.right=0
			end
		end)
		
		if Emulation then
			local keytodirection = {w="forward",a="left",s="backward",d="right"}
			local directionondown = {w=-1,a=-1,s=1,d=1}
			for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name ~= " " then
					v.Changed:connect(function(val)
						controls[keytodirection[v.Name]] = val == true and directionondown[v.Name] or 0
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

end

function onUnequipped()
	equipped=false
end

sp.Equipped:connect(onEquipped)
sp.Unequipped:connect(onUnequipped)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local enabled = true
local playermouse

local range = 90

local cd = 2

local localeffect = plr.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TeleportEffect")

function standUp(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		char.Humanoid.Jump = true
		char.Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
	end
end

function checkIfStanding(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		if char.Humanoid.SeatPart == nil and char.Humanoid.PlatformStand == false then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end

function enable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = true
end

function disable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = false
end

function activate(targetpos,mag)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and enabled then
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if torso and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			disable()
			
			local origin = torso.Position
			local ray1 = Ray.new(origin, (targetpos - origin).unit * mag)
			
			local ignorelist = {}
	
			for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Character then
					table.insert(ignorelist,v.Character)
				end
			end
			
			local hit,destination
			local collision = false
			
			hit,destination = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray1,ignorelist)
			if hit then
				destination = destination - ray1.Unit.Direction * 1.5
				collision = true
			end
			
			if collision then
				local neworigin = targetpos + Vector3.new(0,17,0)
				local ray2 = Ray.new(neworigin, ((neworigin - Vector3.new(0,17,0)) - neworigin).unit * 16)
				local hit2,destination2
				hit2,destination2 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray2, ignorelist)
				if not hit2 then
					destination = targetpos
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayerHasTeleported:FireServer(origin,destination)
			localeffect:Fire(origin,destination)
			if checkIfStanding(char) == false then
				standUp(char)
			end
			wait(.15)
			if torso and checkIfStanding(char) == true then
				torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination)
			end
			
			wait(cd)
			
			enable()
			
		end
	end
end


script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	playermouse = mouse
	equipped = true
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if enabled and equipped then
			local char = plr.Character
			if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				
				local mag = (char.HumanoidRootPart.Position - (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0))).magnitude
				if mag > range then
					mag = range
				end
				local ray = Ray.new(char.HumanoidRootPart.Position, (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag)
				local target = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position + (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag

				activate(target,mag)
			end
			
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()
	playermouse = nil
	equipped = false
end)

		return

local Tool = script.Parent

enabled = true
function onButton1Down(mouse)
	if not enabled then
		return
	end

	enabled = false
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"

	wait(.5)
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	enabled = true

local selectionBox
local selectionLasso
local inGui = false
local inPalette = false

local selectedButtonTable = {}
local oldButton = nil
function onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	if oldButton ~= nil then
		local notSelected = true
		local selectionText = ""
		for key, value in pairs(selectedButtonTable) do
			if oldButton == value then
				notSelected = false
			else
				selectionText = value.BackgroundColor.Name
			end
		end
		if notSelected then
			hoverSelection.Text = selectionText
			oldButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
		end
	end
	oldButton = nil
end

function onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton)
	onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	hoverSelection.Text = guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name
	if guiButton ~= selectedButton then
		guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
		oldButton = guiButton
	end
end

function onMouseUp(colorHolder, paletteFrame, guiButton)
	if selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] ~= nil then
		selectedButtonTable[colorHolder].Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	end
	guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Yellow()
	colorHolder.BackgroundColor = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionBox.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionLasso.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	Tool.Value.Value = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	game.Players.LocalPlayer.HousePaintColor.Value = Tool.Value.Value
	selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] = guiButton
	
	onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame)
	
	print("Selected Color: " .. guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name)
end

function onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame)
	paletteFrame.Visible = true
end

function setSelectionBox(part) 
	unsetSelectionBox()
	selectionBox.Adornee = part.Parent
	selectionLasso.Part = part.Parent.PrimaryPart
end
function unsetSelectionBox() 
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
end

function onMouseEnterGui(mouse)
	mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\ArrowCursor.png"
	unsetSelectionBox()
	inGui = true
end
function onMouseLeaveGui(mouse)
	inGui = false
end

function onMouseEnterPalette(mouse)
	mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\ArrowCursor.png"
	unsetSelectionBox()
	inPalette = true
end
function onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame, mouse)
	paletteFrame.Visible = false
	inPalette = false
end
	

local primaryColor = nil
function buildGui(root, mouse)

	local mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1.0, -200, 1.0, -250)
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.0, 200, 0.0, 250)
	mainFrame.Transparency = 1.0
	mainFrame.Parent = root
	

	local paletteFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	paletteFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, -0.8, 0)
	paletteFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, 0, 8.0/5, 0)
	paletteFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	paletteFrame.Visible = false
	paletteFrame.Parent = mainFrame
	paletteFrame.MouseEnter:connect(function() print("EnterPalette") onMouseEnterPalette(mouse) end)
	paletteFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() print("LeavePalette")  onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame, mouse) end)

	local sideBar = Instance.new("Frame")
	sideBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, 4.0/5, 0)
	sideBar.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, 0, 1.0/5, 0)
	sideBar.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	sideBar.Parent = mainFrame
	sideBar.MouseEnter:connect(function() onMouseEnterGui(mouse) end)
	sideBar.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeaveGui(mouse) end)

	primaryColor = Instance.new("TextButton")
	primaryColor.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 1, 0.0, 1)
	primaryColor.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, -2, 1.0, -2)
	primaryColor.Text  = ""	
	primaryColor.BackgroundColor = Tool.Value.Value
	primaryColor.BorderColor = BrickColor.Black()
	primaryColor.BorderSizePixel = 1
	primaryColor.Parent = sideBar
	primaryColor.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() print("Showdialog") onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame) end)


	local hoverSelection = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	hoverSelection.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 0, 0.0, 0)
	hoverSelection.Size = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 1.0, 0)
	hoverSelection.Text = ""
	hoverSelection.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	hoverSelection.TextColor = BrickColor.White()
	hoverSelection.Text = primaryColor.BackgroundColor.Name
	hoverSelection.Parent = sideBar

	--local secondaryColor = Instance.new("TextButton")
	--secondaryColor.Position = UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 0.0, 0)
	--secondaryColor.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 1.0, 0)
	--secondaryColor.Text = ""
	--secondaryColor.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	--secondaryColor.Parent = sideBar

	for xOffset = 0, 7 do
		for yOffset = 0,15 do
			local guiFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
			guiFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1.0/8 * xOffset, 0, 1.0/16*yOffset, 0)
			guiFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1.0/8, 0, 1.0/16, 0)
			guiFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
			guiFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			guiFrame.Parent = paletteFrame
		
			local guiButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
			guiButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.0, 2, 0.0, 2)
			guiButton.Size = UDim2.new(1.0, -4, 1.0, -4)
			guiButton.Text = ""
			guiButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
			guiButton.AutoButtonColor = false
			guiButton.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.palette(xOffset + yOffset*8)
			guiButton.MouseEnter:connect(function() onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton) end)
			guiButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() onMouseUp(primaryColor, paletteFrame, guiButton) end)
			--guiButton.MouseButton2Down:connect(function() onMouseClick(secondaryColor, guiButton) end)
			guiButton.Parent = guiFrame

			if guiButton.BackgroundColor == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
				guiFrame.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
				selectedButtonTable[primaryColor] = guiButton
			end
		end
	end
	
	local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
		local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
		local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
		
		UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)
			if sideBar and primaryColor and sideBar.Parent ~= nil and paletteFrame and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and not gameprocessed then
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					else
						paletteFrame.Visible = true
						local toselect = primaryColor
						for _,v in pairs(paletteFrame:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Frame") and v:FindFirstChild("TextButton") then
								if v.TextButton.BackgroundColor3 == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
									toselect = v
								end
							end
						end
						GuiService.SelectedObject = toselect
					end
				elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	
	mainFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeave(hoverSelection) end)

end
function canSelectObject(part)
	if part ~= nil then
		return part.Parent and string.sub(part.Parent.Name,1,6) == "furni_" and part.Parent.Parent.Name == "!home_"..game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name and (part.Position - Tool.Parent.Head.Position).Magnitude < 60
	end
end

function canSelectHouseObject(part)
	if part ~= nil then
		return part.Parent and part.Parent.Name == "!home_"..game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name and (part.Position - Tool.Parent.Head.Position).Magnitude < 160
	end
end

function setColor(part)
	if canSelectObject(part) then
		local model = part.Parent
		model.PaintColor.Value = primaryColor.BackgroundColor
		for i,v in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == "Paintable" then
				v.BrickColor = primaryColor.BackgroundColor
			end
		end
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PaintHouseItem:FireServer(model,primaryColor.BackgroundColor)
	elseif canSelectHouseObject(part) then
		for i,v in pairs(part.Parent:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == part.Name then
				v.BrickColor = primaryColor.BackgroundColor
			end
		end
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PaintHouseItem:FireServer(part,primaryColor.BackgroundColor)
	end
end

local mouseDown = false
function on3dButton1Down(mouse) 
	mouseDown = true
	local part = mouse.Target
	setColor(part)
end

function on3dButton1Up(mouse) 
	mouseDown = false
end

function on3dMouseMove(mouse) 
	if not(inGui) and not(inPalette) then
		mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\FillCursor.png"
		local part = mouse.Target
		if canSelectObject(part) then
			setSelectionBox(part)
		else
			unsetSelectionBox()
		end

		if mouseDown then
			setColor(part)
		end
	end
end

local guiMain
function onEquippedLocal(mouse)
	
	local character = script.Parent.Parent
	local player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	
	Tool.Value.Value = player.HousePaintColor.Value

	guiMain = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	guiMain.Name = "PaintBucketGui"
	guiMain.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	inGui = false
	inPalette = false
	mouseDown = false

	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() on3dButton1Down(mouse) end)
	mouse.Button1Up:connect(function() on3dButton1Up(mouse) end)
	mouse.Move:connect(function() on3dMouseMove(mouse) end)
	mouse.Icon ="rbxasset://textures\\FillCursor.png"

	selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
	selectionBox.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionBox.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	selectionLasso = Instance.new("SelectionPartLasso")
	selectionLasso.Name = "Model Delete Lasso"
	selectionLasso.Humanoid = character.Humanoid
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
	selectionLasso.Visible = true
	selectionLasso.archivable = false
	selectionLasso.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionLasso.Parent = game.workspace
	
	buildGui(guiMain, mouse)
end

function onUnequippedLocal()
	guiMain:Remove()
	selectionBox:Remove()
	selectionLasso:Remove()
end

--[[
	// FileName: DPad
	// Version 1.0
	// Written by: jmargh
	// Description: Implements DPad controls for touch devices
--]]

local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local GuiService = game:GetService('GuiService')

local DPad = {}

local MasterControl = require(script.Parent)

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
while not Players.LocalPlayer do
	wait()
end
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local DPadFrame = nil
local TouchObject = nil
local OnInputEnded = nil		-- defined in Create()

--[[ Constants ]]--
local DPAD_SHEET = "rbxasset://textures/ui/DPadSheet.png"
local COMPASS_DIR = {
	Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),			-- E
	Vector3.new(1, 0, 1).unit,		-- SE
	Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),			-- S
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, 1).unit,		-- SW
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),			-- W
	Vector3.new(-1, 0, -1).unit,	-- NW
	Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),			-- N
	Vector3.new(1, 0, -1).unit,		-- NE
}

--[[ lua Function Cache ]]--
local ATAN2 = math.atan2
local FLOOR = math.floor
local PI = math.pi

--[[ Local Functions ]]--
local function createArrowLabel(name, position, size, rectOffset, rectSize)
	local image = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	image.Name = name
	image.Image = DPAD_SHEET
	image.ImageRectOffset = rectOffset
	image.ImageRectSize = rectSize
	image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	image.Size = size
	image.Position = position
	image.Parent = DPadFrame
	
	return image
end

local function getCenterPosition()
	return Vector2.new(DPadFrame.AbsolutePosition.x + DPadFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2, DPadFrame.AbsolutePosition.y + DPadFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
end

--[[ Public API ]]--
function DPad:Enable()
	DPadFrame.Visible = true
end

function DPad:Disable()
	DPadFrame.Visible = false
	OnInputEnded()
end

function DPad:Create(parentFrame)
	if DPadFrame then
		DPadFrame:Destroy()
		DPadFrame = nil
	end
	
	local position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -230)
	DPadFrame = Instance.new('Frame')
	DPadFrame.Name = "DPadFrame"
	DPadFrame.Active = true
	DPadFrame.Visible = false
	DPadFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 192, 0, 192)
	DPadFrame.Position = position
	DPadFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	
	local smArrowSize = UDim2.new(0, 23, 0, 23)
	local lgArrowSize = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
	local smImgOffset = Vector2.new(46, 46)
	local lgImgOffset = Vector2.new(128, 128)
	
	local bBtn = createArrowLabel("BackButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 1, -64), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 0), lgImgOffset)
	local fBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 0, 0), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 258), lgImgOffset)
	local lBtn = createArrowLabel("LeftButton", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 129), lgImgOffset)
	local rBtn = createArrowLabel("RightButton", UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(0, 129), lgImgOffset)
	local jumpBtn = createArrowLabel("JumpButton", UDim2.new(0.5, -32, 0.5, -32), lgArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 0), lgImgOffset)
	local flBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardLeftButton", UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 35), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(129, 258), smImgOffset)
	local frBtn = createArrowLabel("ForwardRightButton", UDim2.new(1, -55, 0, 35), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(176, 258), smImgOffset)
	flBtn.Visible = false
	frBtn.Visible = false
	
	-- input connections
	jumpBtn.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		MasterControl:DoJump()
	end)
	
	local movementVector = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
	local function normalizeDirection(inputPosition)
		local jumpRadius = jumpBtn.AbsoluteSize.x/2
		local centerPosition = getCenterPosition()
		local direction = Vector2.new(inputPosition.x - centerPosition.x, inputPosition.y - centerPosition.y)
		
		if direction.magnitude > jumpRadius then
			local angle = ATAN2(direction.y, direction.x)
			local octant = (FLOOR(8 * angle / (2 * PI) + 8.5)%8) + 1
			movementVector = COMPASS_DIR[octant]
		end
		
		if not flBtn.Visible and movementVector == COMPASS_DIR[7] then
			flBtn.Visible = true
			frBtn.Visible = true
		end
	end
	
	DPadFrame.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		if TouchObject or inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
		
		TouchObject = inputObject
		normalizeDirection(TouchObject.Position)
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(movementVector)
	end)
	
	DPadFrame.InputChanged:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
			normalizeDirection(TouchObject.Position)
			MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(movementVector)
			MasterControl:SetIsJumping(false)
		end
	end)
	
	OnInputEnded = function()
		TouchObject = nil
		flBtn.Visible = false
		frBtn.Visible = false
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-movementVector)
		movementVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
	
	DPadFrame.InputEnded:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	GuiService.MenuOpened:connect(function()
		if TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	DPadFrame.Parent = parentFrame
end

return DPad

	local image = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	image.Name = name
	image.Image = DPAD_SHEET
	image.ImageRectOffset = rectOffset
	image.ImageRectSize = rectSize
	image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	image.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(190/255, 190/255, 190/255)
	image.Size = size
	image.Position = position
	image.Parent = parent
	
	return image
end

--[[ Public API ]]--
function Thumbpad:Enable()
	ThumbpadFrame.Visible = true
end

function Thumbpad:Disable()
	ThumbpadFrame.Visible = false
	OnInputEnded()
end

function Thumbpad:Create(parentFrame)
	if ThumbpadFrame then
		ThumbpadFrame:Destroy()
		ThumbpadFrame = nil
		if OnTouchChangedCn then
			OnTouchChangedCn:disconnect()
			OnTouchChangedCn = nil
		end
		if OnTouchEndedCn then
			OnTouchEndedCn:disconnect()
			OnTouchEndedCn = nil
		end
	end
	
	local isSmallScreen = parentFrame.AbsoluteSize.y <= 500
	local thumbpadSize = isSmallScreen and 70 or 120
	local position = isSmallScreen and UDim2.new(0, thumbpadSize * 1.25, 1, -thumbpadSize - 20) or
		UDim2.new(0, thumbpadSize/2 - 10, 1, -thumbpadSize * 1.75 - 10)
	
	ThumbpadFrame = Instance.new('Frame')
	ThumbpadFrame.Name = "ThumbpadFrame"
	ThumbpadFrame.Visible = false
	ThumbpadFrame.Active = true
	ThumbpadFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbpadSize + 20, 0, thumbpadSize + 20)
	ThumbpadFrame.Position = position
	ThumbpadFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	
	local outerImage = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	outerImage.Name = "OuterImage"
	outerImage.Image = TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET
	outerImage.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	outerImage.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(220, 220)
	outerImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	outerImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbpadSize, 0, thumbpadSize)
	outerImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
	outerImage.Parent = ThumbpadFrame
	
	local smArrowSize = isSmallScreen and UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32) or UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
	local lgArrowSize = UDim2.new(0, smArrowSize.X.Offset * 2, 0, smArrowSize.Y.Offset * 2)
	local imgRectSize = Vector2.new(110, 110)
	local smImgOffset = isSmallScreen and -4 or -9
	local lgImgOffset = isSmallScreen and -28 or -55
	
	local dArrow = createArrowLabel("DownArrow", outerImage, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 1, lgImgOffset), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(8, 8), imgRectSize)
	local uArrow = createArrowLabel("UpArrow", outerImage, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 0, smImgOffset), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(8, 266), imgRectSize)
	local lArrow = createArrowLabel("LeftArrow", outerImage, UDim2.new(0, smImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(137, 137), imgRectSize)
	local rArrow = createArrowLabel("RightArrow", outerImage, UDim2.new(1, lgImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2), smArrowSize, Vector2.new(8, 137), imgRectSize)
	
	local function doTween(guiObject, endSize, endPosition)
		guiObject:TweenSizeAndPosition(endSize, endPosition, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, 0.15, true)
	end
	
	local padOrigin = nil
	local deadZone = 0.1
	local isRight, isLeft, isUp, isDown = false, false, false, false
	local vForward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	local vRight = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
	local function doMove(pos)
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-currentMoveVector)
		
		local delta = Vector2.new(pos.x, pos.y) - padOrigin
		currentMoveVector = delta / (thumbpadSize/2)
		
		-- Scaled Radial Dead Zone
		local inputAxisMagnitude = currentMoveVector.magnitude
		if inputAxisMagnitude < deadZone then
			currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		else
			currentMoveVector = currentMoveVector.unit * ((inputAxisMagnitude - deadZone) / (1 - deadZone))
			-- catch possible NAN Vector
			if currentMoveVector.magnitude == 0 then
				currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			else
				currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(currentMoveVector.x, 0, currentMoveVector.y).unit
			end
		end
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(currentMoveVector)
		
		local forwardDot = currentMoveVector:Dot(vForward)
		local rightDot = currentMoveVector:Dot(vRight)
		if forwardDot > 0.5 then		-- UP
			if not isUp then
				isUp, isDown = true, false
				doTween(uArrow, lgArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset, 0, smImgOffset - smArrowSize.Y.Offset * 1.5))
				doTween(dArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 1, lgImgOffset))
			end
		elseif forwardDot < -0.5 then	-- DOWN
			if not isDown then
				isDown, isUp = true, false
				doTween(dArrow, lgArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset, 1, lgImgOffset + smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
				doTween(uArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 0, smImgOffset))
			end
		else
			isUp, isDown = false, false
			doTween(dArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 1, lgImgOffset))
			doTween(uArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 0, smImgOffset))
		end
		
		if rightDot > 0.5 then
			if not isRight then
				isRight, isLeft = true, false
				doTween(rArrow, lgArrowSize, UDim2.new(1, lgImgOffset + smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset))
				doTween(lArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0, smImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
			end
		elseif rightDot < -0.5 then
			if not isLeft then
				isLeft, isRight = true, false
				doTween(lArrow, lgArrowSize, UDim2.new(0, smImgOffset - smArrowSize.X.Offset * 1.5, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset))
				doTween(rArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(1, lgImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
			end
		else
			isRight, isLeft = false, false
			doTween(lArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0, smImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
			doTween(rArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(1, lgImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
		end
	end
	
	--input connections
	ThumbpadFrame.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		if TouchObject or inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		
		ThumbpadFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, inputObject.Position.x - ThumbpadFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2, 0, inputObject.Position.y - ThumbpadFrame.Size.Y.Offset/2)
		padOrigin = Vector2.new(ThumbpadFrame.AbsolutePosition.x + ThumbpadFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2,
			ThumbpadFrame.AbsolutePosition.y + ThumbpadFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
		doMove(inputObject.Position)
		TouchObject = inputObject
	end)
	
	OnTouchChangedCn = UserInputService.TouchMoved:connect(function(inputObject, isProcessed)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			doMove(TouchObject.Position)
		end
	end)
	
	OnInputEnded = function()
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-currentMoveVector)
		currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		MasterControl:SetIsJumping(false)

		ThumbpadFrame.Position = position
		TouchObject = nil
		isUp, isDown, isLeft, isRight = false, false, false, false
		doTween(dArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 1, lgImgOffset))
		doTween(uArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0.5, -smArrowSize.X.Offset/2, 0, smImgOffset))
		doTween(lArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(0, smImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
		doTween(rArrow, smArrowSize, UDim2.new(1, lgImgOffset, 0.5, -smArrowSize.Y.Offset/2))
	end
	
	OnTouchEndedCn = UserInputService.TouchEnded:connect(function(inputObject)
		if inputObject == TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	GuiService.MenuOpened:connect(function()
		if TouchObject then
			OnInputEnded()
		end
	end)	
	
	ThumbpadFrame.Parent = parentFrame
end

return Thumbpad

--[[
	// FileName: Thumbstick
	// Version 1.0
	// Written by: jmargh
	// Description: Implements thumbstick controls for touch devices
--]]
local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local UserInputService = game:GetService('UserInputService')
local GuiService = game:GetService('GuiService')

local MasterControl = require(script.Parent)

local Thumbstick = {}

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
while not Players.LocalPlayer do
	wait()
end
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local IsFollowStick = false
local ThumbstickFrame = nil
local MoveTouchObject = nil
local OnTouchEnded = nil		-- defined in Create()
local OnTouchMovedCn = nil
local OnTouchEndedCn = nil
local currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(0,0,0)

--[[ Constants ]]--
local TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET = "rbxasset://textures/ui/TouchControlsSheet.png"

--[[ Public API ]]--
function Thumbstick:Enable()
	ThumbstickFrame.Visible = true
end

function Thumbstick:Disable()
	ThumbstickFrame.Visible = false
	OnTouchEnded()
end

function Thumbstick:Create(parentFrame)
	if ThumbstickFrame then
		ThumbstickFrame:Destroy()
		ThumbstickFrame = nil
		if OnTouchMovedCn then
			OnTouchMovedCn:disconnect()
			OnTouchMovedCn = nil
		end
		if OnTouchEndedCn then
			OnTouchEndedCn:disconnect()
			OnTouchEndedCn = nil
		end
	end
		
	local isSmallScreen = parentFrame.AbsoluteSize.y <= 500
	local thumbstickSize = isSmallScreen and 70 or 120
	local position = isSmallScreen and UDim2.new(0, (thumbstickSize/2) - 10, 1, -thumbstickSize - 20) or
		UDim2.new(0, thumbstickSize/2, 1, -thumbstickSize * 1.75)
		
	ThumbstickFrame = Instance.new('Frame')
	ThumbstickFrame.Name = "ThumbstickFrame"
	ThumbstickFrame.Active = true
	ThumbstickFrame.Visible = false
	ThumbstickFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbstickSize, 0, thumbstickSize)
	ThumbstickFrame.Position = position
	ThumbstickFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	
	local outerImage = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	outerImage.Name = "OuterImage"
	outerImage.Image = TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET
	outerImage.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new()
	outerImage.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(220, 220)
	outerImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	outerImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbstickSize, 0, thumbstickSize)
	outerImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	outerImage.Parent = ThumbstickFrame
	
	StickImage = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
	StickImage.Name = "StickImage"
	StickImage.Image = TOUCH_CONTROL_SHEET
	StickImage.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(220, 0)
	StickImage.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(111, 111)
	StickImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	StickImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, thumbstickSize/2, 0, thumbstickSize/2)
	StickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, thumbstickSize/2 - thumbstickSize/4, 0, thumbstickSize/2 - thumbstickSize/4)
	StickImage.ZIndex = 2
	StickImage.Parent = ThumbstickFrame
	
	local centerPosition = nil
	local deadZone = 0.05
	local function doMove(direction)
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-currentMoveVector)
		
		currentMoveVector = direction / (thumbstickSize/2)
		
		-- Scaled Radial Dead Zone
		local inputAxisMagnitude = currentMoveVector.magnitude
		if inputAxisMagnitude < deadZone then
			currentMoveVector = Vector3.new()
		else
			currentMoveVector = currentMoveVector.unit * ((inputAxisMagnitude - deadZone) / (1 - deadZone))
			-- NOTE: Making currentMoveVector a unit vector will cause the player to instantly go max speed
			-- must check for zero length vector is using unit
			currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(currentMoveVector.x, 0, currentMoveVector.y)
		end
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(currentMoveVector)
	end
	
	local function moveStick(pos)
		local relativePosition = Vector2.new(pos.x - centerPosition.x, pos.y - centerPosition.y)
		local length = relativePosition.magnitude
		local maxLength = ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2
		if IsFollowStick and length > maxLength then
			local offset = relativePosition.unit * maxLength
			ThumbstickFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
				0, pos.x - ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2 - offset.x,
				0, pos.y - ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2 - offset.y)
		else
			length = math.min(length, maxLength)
			relativePosition = relativePosition.unit * length
		end
		StickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, relativePosition.x + StickImage.AbsoluteSize.x/2, 0, relativePosition.y + StickImage.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
	end
	
	-- input connections
	ThumbstickFrame.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
		if MoveTouchObject or inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end
		
		MoveTouchObject = inputObject
		ThumbstickFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, inputObject.Position.x - ThumbstickFrame.Size.X.Offset/2, 0, inputObject.Position.y - ThumbstickFrame.Size.Y.Offset/2)
		centerPosition = Vector2.new(ThumbstickFrame.AbsolutePosition.x + ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2,
			ThumbstickFrame.AbsolutePosition.y + ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
		local direction = Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.x - centerPosition.x, inputObject.Position.y - centerPosition.y)
	end)
	
	OnTouchMovedCn = UserInputService.TouchMoved:connect(function(inputObject, isProcessed)
		if inputObject == MoveTouchObject then
			centerPosition = Vector2.new(ThumbstickFrame.AbsolutePosition.x + ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.x/2,
				ThumbstickFrame.AbsolutePosition.y + ThumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.y/2)
			local direction = Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.x - centerPosition.x, inputObject.Position.y - centerPosition.y)
			doMove(direction)
			moveStick(inputObject.Position)
		end
	end)
	
	OnTouchEnded = function()
		ThumbstickFrame.Position = position
		StickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, ThumbstickFrame.Size.X.Offset/2 - thumbstickSize/4, 0, ThumbstickFrame.Size.Y.Offset/2 - thumbstickSize/4)
		MoveTouchObject = nil
		
		MasterControl:AddToPlayerMovement(-currentMoveVector)
		currentMoveVector = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		MasterControl:SetIsJumping(false)
	end
	
	OnTouchEndedCn = UserInputService.TouchEnded:connect(function(inputObject, isProcessed)
		if inputObject == MoveTouchObject then
			OnTouchEnded()
		end
	end)
	
	GuiService.MenuOpened:connect(function()
		if MoveTouchObject then
			OnTouchEnded()
		end
	end)	
	
	ThumbstickFrame.Parent = parentFrame
end

if game:GetService("UserInputService").TouchEnabled and not game:GetService("UserInputService").KeyboardEnabled and not game:GetService("UserInputService").GamepadEnabled then
	local Emulation = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	local w
	local a
	local s
	local d
	local space
	local MouseIsDown
	if Emulation then
		w = Emulation:WaitForChild("w")
		a = Emulation:WaitForChild("a")
		s = Emulation:WaitForChild("s")
		d = Emulation:WaitForChild("d")
		space = Emulation:WaitForChild(" ")
		MouseIsDown = Emulation:WaitForChild("MouseIsDown")
	else
		Emulation = Instance.new("Folder")
		Emulation.Name = "KeyboardEmulation"
		w = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		w.Name = "w"
		a = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		a.Name = "a"
		s = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		s.Name = "s"
		d = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		d.Name = "d"
		space = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		space.Name = " "
		MouseIsDown = Instance.new("BoolValue",Emulation)
		MouseIsDown.Name = "MouseIsDown"
		Emulation.Parent = LocalPlayer
	end
	
	game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:connect(function()
		if Emulation and Emulation.Parent ~= nil then
			local vec = currentMoveVector
			local x = vec.x
			local z = vec.z
			local greatest = 0
			if math.abs(x) > greatest then
				greatest = math.abs(x)
			end
			if math.abs(z) > greatest then
				greatest = math.abs(z)
			end
			if greatest > 1 then
				vec = vec / greatest
			end
			-- negative z is up, positive z is down. x is normal
			w.Value = vec.z < -.58 and true or false
			a.Value = vec.x < -.58 and true or false
			s.Value = vec.z > .58 and true or false
			d.Value = vec.x > .58 and true or false
		end
	end)
	
	UserInputService.TouchStarted:connect(function()
		if Emulation and Emulation.Parent ~= nil then
			MouseIsDown.Value = true
		end
	end)
	
	UserInputService.TouchEnded:connect(function()
		if Emulation and Emulation.Parent ~= nil then
			MouseIsDown.Value = false
		end
	end)
end

return Thumbstick
